# ---------- ASCII-coded numeric types ----------
# Numbers written as text in a fixed-width field (NITF BCS-N, FITS card values,
# USGSDEM Fortran fields). The parsed HEL value is numeric, so these fields may
# drive sizing, offset and repeat expressions — which bddo:string cannot.
:asciiInteger a owl:NamedIndividual , :DataType ; rdfs:label "asciiInteger"@en ; :baseType :baseInteger ; :encoding :ascii ; :xsdType xsd:integer ;
    rdfs:comment "An integer written as text, occupying the field's declared width (bddo:size) or ending at its terminator. Surrounding space and a leading '+'/'-' are accepted. Radix is bddo:numericBase, default 10. Yields a HEL Integer."@en .
:asciiDecimal a owl:NamedIndividual , :DataType ; rdfs:label "asciiDecimal"@en ; :baseType :baseFloat ; :encoding :ascii ; :xsdType xsd:decimal ;
    rdfs:comment "A fixed- or floating-point number written as text, occupying the field's declared width or ending at its terminator. Accepts leading sign, decimal point and an exponent introduced by E, e, D or d -- Fortran writes a double-precision exponent with D, and formats written by Fortran (USGSDEM record A) use it. A value spelled 'nan' (any case, optional leading sign) reads as a not-a-number; an infinite value is not accepted. Yields a HEL Float."@en .

# ---------- Variable-length integers ----------
# A varint's width is neither intrinsic (as with uint32) nor declared (as with
# asciiInteger's bddo:size): it is DISCOVERED while reading, from the values of the bytes
# themselves. These are therefore the first data types with no width at all, and a field
# using one must not declare a size -- there is nothing for a size to mean, and stating one
# would contradict the bytes. sizeof() on such a field still yields the length actually
# consumed, once it is known.
#
# They are what modern container formats are built from: protobuf key/length prefixes (MVT,
# OSM PBF), the PMTiles directory, Parquet's Thrift-compact footer, FlatGeobuf and
# OpenFileGDB geometry. Framing those was already possible byte by byte; reconstructing the
# VALUE was not, which is what kept them out of reach.
:varuint a owl:NamedIndividual , :DataType ; rdfs:label "varuint"@en ; :baseType :baseInteger ; :isSigned false ; :xsdType xsd:nonNegativeInteger ;
    rdfs:comment "Unsigned base-128 varint (LEB128): 7 value bits per byte, little-endian, the high bit set on every byte but the last. Protobuf's plain varint."@en .
:varint a owl:NamedIndividual , :DataType ; rdfs:label "varint"@en ; :baseType :baseInteger ; :isSigned true ; :xsdType xsd:integer ;
    rdfs:comment "Signed base-128 varint: LEB128 carrying a zigzag-encoded value, so small negatives stay short -- n maps to (n << 1) XOR (n >> 63). Protobuf's sint32/sint64. Distinct from reading an unsigned varint and reinterpreting the bits, which is protobuf's int32 and sign-extends instead."@en .
:sqliteVarint a owl:NamedIndividual , :DataType ; rdfs:label "sqliteVarint"@en ; :baseType :baseInteger ; :isSigned true ; :xsdType xsd:integer ;
    rdfs:comment "SQLite's variable-length integer: one to nine BIG-endian bytes, high bit as continuation for the first eight, the ninth contributing all eight bits. A different scheme from LEB128, not a dialect of it; used by SQLite-backed formats (GeoPackage, MBTiles, Rasterlite)."@en .

# =========================================================
# SHACL shapes (domain + cardinality; refine the declared ranges)
# =========================================================
bddo:_prefixes sh:declare
    [ sh:prefix "bddo" ; sh:namespace "https://hexplain.io/ns/bddo#"^^xsd:anyURI ] ,
    [ sh:prefix "rdf" ; sh:namespace "http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#"^^xsd:anyURI ] ,
    [ sh:prefix "rdfs" ; sh:namespace "http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#"^^xsd:anyURI ] .

bddo:StructShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Struct ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasField ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:BlankNodeOrIRI ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasField must point to a single rdf:List." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasField [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:class bddo:Field ] [ sh:class bddo:LocalBinding ] ) ;
        sh:message "Every member of a Struct's hasField list must be a bddo:Field or a bddo:LocalBinding." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:endianness ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( bddo:BigEndian bddo:LittleEndian ) ; sh:message "bddo:endianness takes at most one value; each is either bddo:BigEndian or bddo:LittleEndian." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:bitOrder ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( bddo:MSBFirst bddo:LSBFirst ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:bitOrder takes at most one value; each is either bddo:MSBFirst or bddo:LSBFirst." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:syncOnMarker ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ;
        sh:message "bddo:syncOnMarker takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    # Struct-level size bounds the byte extent of the Struct (a bounded region for its children).
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:size ; sh:minExclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:size takes at most one value; each is a number greater than 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:sizeFromField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromExpression ; sh:or ( [ sh:datatype xsd:string ] [ sh:datatype bddo:HelExpression ] ) ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:sizeFromExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] .

# At most one struct-sizing mechanism per Struct.
bddo:StructSizingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Struct ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:Struct must use at most one sizing mechanism (size / sizeFromField / sizeFromExpression)." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:size, bddo:sizeFromField, bddo:sizeFromExpression))
        } GROUP BY $this HAVING (COUNT(DISTINCT ?p) > 1)""" ] .

bddo:FieldShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:or (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dataType ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasConditionalDataType ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:valueFromExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasDispatchTable ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
    ) ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dataType ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:class bddo:DataType ] [ sh:class bddo:Struct ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:dataType takes at most one value; each is an IRI naming an instance of bddo:DataType or bddo:Struct." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasConditionalDataType [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:class bddo:DataTypeRule ;
        sh:message "Every member of a hasConditionalDataType list must be a bddo:DataTypeRule." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasDispatchTable ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:class bddo:DispatchTable ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasDispatchTable takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:DispatchTable." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:seekScope ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( bddo:regionScope bddo:streamScope ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:seekScope takes at most one value; each is either bddo:regionScope or bddo:streamScope." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:size ; sh:minExclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:size takes at most one value; each is a number greater than 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:sizeFromField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromExpression ; sh:or ( [ sh:datatype xsd:string ] [ sh:datatype bddo:HelExpression ] ) ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:sizeFromExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeToEndOfStream ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:sizeToEndOfStream takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:terminator ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:terminator takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( "whitespace" ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:separatedBy takes at most one value; each is an xsd:string literal equal to \"whitespace\"." ] ;
    # bddo:separatedBy IS the field's extent, so it excludes every other way of stating one.
    # All five, not just the two literal spellings: a separated field declaring
    # sizeFromField/sizeFromExpression/sizeToEndOfStream would otherwise satisfy both this shape
    # and bddo:AsciiNumericWidthShape while asserting a width the reader provably ignores
    # (a conforming processor resolves bddo:separatedBy before any size declaration), which is a false
    # statement that validates.
    sh:not [ sh:and (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:size ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:and (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:and (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeFromExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:and (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:sizeToEndOfStream ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:and (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:separatedBy ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:terminator ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:trimNull ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:trimNull takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:encoding ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( bddo:utf8 bddo:ascii bddo:utf16le bddo:utf16be bddo:latin1 ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:encoding takes at most one value; each is one of bddo:utf8, bddo:ascii, bddo:utf16le, bddo:utf16be or bddo:latin1." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:bitLength ; sh:minExclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:bitLength takes at most one value; each is a number greater than 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:alignment ; sh:minExclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:alignment takes at most one value; each is a number greater than 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:atOffset ; sh:minInclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:atOffset takes at most one value; each is a number at least 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:atOffsetFromField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:atOffsetFromField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:atOffsetFromExpression ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:atOffsetFromExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:offsetBase ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:streamStart bddo:streamEnd bddo:parentStart bddo:currentPosition ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:offsetBase takes at most one value; each is one of bddo:streamStart, bddo:streamEnd, bddo:parentStart or bddo:currentPosition." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:enumeration ; sh:class bddo:Enumeration ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:enumeration takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Enumeration." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:checksum ; sh:class bddo:Checksum ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:checksum takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Checksum." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:valueFromExpression ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:valueFromExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:validIf ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:validIf takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:rootKeyFromField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:rootKeyFromField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:isPresentIf ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:isPresentIf takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] .

# At most one sizing mechanism per field.
bddo:FieldSizingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:Field must use at most one sizing/termination mechanism." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:size, bddo:sizeFromField, bddo:sizeFromExpression,
                          bddo:sizeToEndOfStream, bddo:terminator))
        } GROUP BY $this HAVING (COUNT(DISTINCT ?p) > 1)""" ] .

# A variable-length field (bytes/string) MUST declare exactly one sizing/termination.
# Exempt: a field of a delimited-record container gets its extent from the container's
# recordDelimiter/fieldDelimiter, not from its own size or terminator.
bddo:VariableLengthFieldShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:bytes or bddo:string field must declare a sizing or termination mechanism." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:dataType ?dt . FILTER(?dt IN (bddo:bytes, bddo:string))
            FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                $this ?p ?v .
                FILTER(?p IN (bddo:size, bddo:sizeFromField, bddo:sizeFromExpression,
                              bddo:sizeToEndOfStream, bddo:terminator))
            }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                ?p bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this .
                ?p a ?c .
                # Every container form that determines a field's extent ITSELF -- a delimited
                # record ends at its delimiter, a tree node at its own boundary -- so a declared
                # width would be a second, contradictory answer. Enumerated rather than inferred
                # via rdfs:subClassOf, because these shapes run without inference; a new
                # container form has to be added here too.
                FILTER(?c IN (bddo:DelimitedRecords, bddo:KeyValueHeader, bddo:DelimitedTable,
                              bddo:TreeDocument))
            }
        }""" ] .

# At most one repetition mechanism per field.
bddo:FieldRepetitionShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:Field must use at most one repetition mechanism." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:repeatCount, bddo:repeatCountFromField,
                          bddo:repeatCountFromExpression, bddo:repeatUntil))
        } GROUP BY $this HAVING (COUNT(DISTINCT ?p) > 1)""" ] .

# At most one offset mechanism per field.
bddo:FieldOffsetShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:Field must use at most one offset mechanism." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:atOffset, bddo:atOffsetFromField, bddo:atOffsetFromExpression))
        } GROUP BY $this HAVING (COUNT(DISTINCT ?p) > 1)""" ] .

# A derived (zero-byte) field computes its value from an expression and MUST NOT
# also consume bytes via a data type, sizing, offset, bit-length or enumeration.
bddo:DerivedFieldShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:valueFromExpression (derived) field must not also declare a byte-consuming mechanism (dataType, hasConditionalDataType, size*, terminator, bitLength, atOffset* or enumeration)." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:valueFromExpression ?e .
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:dataType, bddo:hasConditionalDataType, bddo:size,
                          bddo:sizeFromField, bddo:sizeFromExpression, bddo:sizeToEndOfStream,
                          bddo:terminator, bddo:bitLength, bddo:atOffset, bddo:atOffsetFromField,
                          bddo:atOffsetFromExpression, bddo:enumeration))
        }""" ] .

bddo:DataTypeShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DataType ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:baseType ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:baseInteger bddo:baseFloat bddo:baseString bddo:baseBytes ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:baseType takes at most one value; each is one of bddo:baseInteger, bddo:baseFloat, bddo:baseString or bddo:baseBytes." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:bitWidth ; sh:minExclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:bitWidth takes at most one value; each is a number greater than 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:isSigned ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:isSigned takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:endianness ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( bddo:BigEndian bddo:LittleEndian ) ; sh:message "bddo:endianness takes at most one value; each is either bddo:BigEndian or bddo:LittleEndian." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:xsdType ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:xsdType takes at most one value; each is an IRI." ] .

bddo:DataTypeRuleShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DataTypeRule ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:condition ; sh:or ( [ sh:datatype xsd:string ] [ sh:datatype bddo:HelExpression ] ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:condition takes exactly one value, a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:ruleDataType ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:class bddo:DataType ] [ sh:class bddo:Struct ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:ruleDataType takes exactly one value, an IRI naming an instance of bddo:DataType or bddo:Struct." ] .

bddo:DispatchTableShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DispatchTable ;
    sh:xone (
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dispatchOnField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
        [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dispatchOnExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
    ) ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dispatchOnField ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:class bddo:Field ;
        sh:message "bddo:dispatchOnField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dispatchOnExpression ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ;
        sh:message "bddo:dispatchOnExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dispatchDefault ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:class bddo:DataType ] [ sh:class bddo:Struct ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:dispatchDefault takes at most one value; each is an IRI naming an instance of bddo:DataType or bddo:Struct." ] .

bddo:DispatchArmShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DispatchArm ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:armTable , bddo:armKey , bddo:armDataType ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:armTable ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:class bddo:DispatchTable ;
        sh:message "bddo:armTable takes exactly one value, an instance of bddo:DispatchTable." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:armKey ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:datatype xsd:string ] [ sh:datatype xsd:integer ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:armKey takes exactly one value, an xsd:string or xsd:integer literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:armDataType ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:class bddo:DataType ] [ sh:class bddo:Struct ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:armDataType takes exactly one value, an IRI naming an instance of bddo:DataType or bddo:Struct." ] .

# Keys are unique per table across every contributing graph: two arms with one key would make
# the selection depend on graph order, which is exactly what a keyed table exists to rule out.
bddo:DispatchKeyUniquenessShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DispatchTable ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "Two DispatchArms of one DispatchTable declare the same armKey." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?key WHERE {
            ?a bddo:armTable $this ; bddo:armKey ?key .
            ?b bddo:armTable $this ; bddo:armKey ?key .
            FILTER(?a != ?b)
        }""" ] .

# A field selects its type one way: keyed (open, disjoint) or ordered predicates (closed).
bddo:TypeSelectionExclusivityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:Field must not declare both hasDispatchTable and hasConditionalDataType." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:hasDispatchTable ?t ; bddo:hasConditionalDataType ?c .
        }""" ] .

# seekScope describes an offset-addressed read; on a sequential field it describes nothing.
bddo:SeekScopeShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "bddo:seekScope is only meaningful on an offset-addressed Field (atOffset / atOffsetFromField / atOffsetFromExpression)." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:seekScope ?s .
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this ?p ?v . FILTER(?p IN (bddo:atOffset, bddo:atOffsetFromField, bddo:atOffsetFromExpression)) }
        }""" ] .

bddo:EnumerationShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Enumeration ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasEnumValue ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:class bddo:EnumValue ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasEnumValue takes at least one value; each is an instance of bddo:EnumValue." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:enumIsFlags ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:enumIsFlags takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] .

bddo:EnumValueShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:EnumValue ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:enumRawValue ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:enumRawValue takes exactly one value." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:enumSymbol ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:enumSymbol takes at most one value; each is an IRI." ] .

bddo:ChecksumShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Checksum ;
    sh:targetObjectsOf bddo:checksum ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:checksumAlgorithm ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or ( [ sh:in ( bddo:crc16 bddo:crc32 bddo:adler32 bddo:md5 bddo:sha1 bddo:sha256 bddo:crc8Smbus bddo:crc16Arc bddo:crc16Xmodem bddo:crc16Modbus bddo:crc16X25 bddo:crc32c bddo:crc32Bzip2 bddo:crc32Mpeg2 bddo:crc64Ecma182 bddo:crc64Xz ) ]
                [ sh:class bddo:CustomCrc ] ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:checksumAlgorithm is one of the named algorithms or a bddo:CustomCrc." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:coversFromField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToField ; sh:class bddo:Field ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:coversToField takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:Field." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversExpression ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:coversExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromExpression ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:coversFromExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToExpression ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:coversToExpression takes at most one value; each is a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    # One mechanism per bound. Both would leave the reader to guess which wins, and the two
    # forms do not even agree on whether the end byte is included, so the guess changes the
    # bytes hashed.
    sh:not [ sh:and ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                      [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:and ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                      [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ;
    # The deprecated whole-range form describes both bounds by itself, so pairing it with a
    # per-bound property describes the same bound twice.
    sh:not [ sh:and ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                      [ sh:or ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                                [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToField ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                                [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversFromExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                                [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:coversToExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ] ] ) ] ) ] .

# ---------- Custom CRC ----------
bddo:CustomCrcShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:CustomCrc ;
    # Datatypes and non-negativity come from bddo:RangeDatatypeShape, which applies wherever the
    # properties are used; this shape adds presence, uniqueness and the width's bounds.
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcWidth ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:minInclusive 8 ; sh:maxInclusive 64 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcWidth, an integer from 8 to 64." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcPolynomial ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcPolynomial." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcInit ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcInit." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcXorOut ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcXorOut." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcReflectIn ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcReflectIn." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:crcReflectOut ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:CustomCrc states exactly one bddo:crcReflectOut." ] ;
    # Each register value fits the register: below 2^width. SPARQL has neither a power function
    # nor, in a SHACL constraint, a VALUES table, so 2^width is 256^k * 2^r for width = 8k + r.
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "{?p} {?v} does not fit a {?w}-bit CRC: it must be below 2^{?w}." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?p ?v ?w WHERE {
            $this bddo:crcWidth ?w ; ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:crcPolynomial, bddo:crcInit, bddo:crcXorOut))
            FILTER(isNumeric(?w) && isNumeric(?v) && ?w >= 8 && ?w <= 64)
            BIND(FLOOR(?w / 8) AS ?k)
            BIND(?w - 8 * ?k AS ?r)
            BIND(IF(?k = 1, 256, IF(?k = 2, 65536, IF(?k = 3, 16777216, IF(?k = 4, 4294967296,
                 IF(?k = 5, 1099511627776, IF(?k = 6, 281474976710656, IF(?k = 7, 72057594037927936,
                 18446744073709551616))))))) AS ?bytes)
            BIND(IF(?r = 0, 1, IF(?r = 1, 2, IF(?r = 2, 4, IF(?r = 3, 8, IF(?r = 4, 16, IF(?r = 5, 32,
                 IF(?r = 6, 64, 128))))))) AS ?bits)
            FILTER(?v >= ?bytes * ?bits)
        }""" ] .

# ---------- Parameterised structs ----------
bddo:ParameterisedStructShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:hasParameter ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasParameter ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:BlankNodeOrIRI ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasParameter must point to a single rdf:List." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasParameter [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:class bddo:Parameter ; sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:message "Every member of a hasParameter list is a bddo:Parameter named by its IRI." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path rdf:type ; sh:hasValue bddo:Struct ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasParameter is declared on a bddo:Struct." ] .

bddo:ParameterShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Parameter ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:parameterType ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:parameterType ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:IntegerParameter bddo:FloatParameter bddo:StringParameter bddo:BytesParameter bddo:BooleanParameter ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:parameterType is one of bddo:IntegerParameter, bddo:FloatParameter, bddo:StringParameter, bddo:BytesParameter and bddo:BooleanParameter." ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:class bddo:Field ] .

bddo:ArgumentListShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:hasArgument ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasArgument ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:BlankNodeOrIRI ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasArgument must point to a single rdf:List." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasArgument [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ;
        sh:message "Every member of a hasArgument list is a HEL expression, an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal." ] .

# Arity: every place that names a struct as a type supplies one argument per parameter. List
# NODES are counted, not members, so two equal argument strings still count twice.
bddo:ArgumentArityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    rdfs:comment "Wherever a bddo:Struct that declares parameters is named as a type (by bddo:dataType, bddo:ruleDataType or bddo:armDataType), the naming node supplies exactly one bddo:hasArgument list member per parameter. List nodes are counted, so two equal argument strings count twice."@en ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:dataType , bddo:ruleDataType , bddo:armDataType ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "{?s} declares {?np} parameter(s), but {?na} argument(s) are supplied for it." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?s (COUNT(DISTINCT ?pn) AS ?np) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?an) AS ?na) WHERE {
            $this ?tp ?s .
            FILTER(?tp IN (bddo:dataType, bddo:ruleDataType, bddo:armDataType))
            ?s a bddo:Struct .
            OPTIONAL { ?s bddo:hasParameter/rdf:rest* ?pn . FILTER(?pn != rdf:nil) }
            OPTIONAL { $this bddo:hasArgument/rdf:rest* ?an . FILTER(?an != rdf:nil) }
        } GROUP BY $this ?s HAVING (COUNT(DISTINCT ?pn) != COUNT(DISTINCT ?an))""" ] .

# ---------- Local bindings ----------
bddo:LocalBindingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:LocalBinding ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:localExpression ;
    sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:localExpression ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:node bddo:HelExpressionValueShape ;
        sh:message "A bddo:LocalBinding states exactly one bddo:localExpression." ] ;
    sh:not [ sh:class bddo:Field ] ;
    # A text container locates its fields by delimiter, key or path rather than by list order,
    # so a binding in one would have no position to be evaluated at.
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:LocalBinding is a member of a binary struct; {?container} is a delimited or tree container." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?container WHERE {
            ?container bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this ; a ?c .
            FILTER(?c IN (bddo:DelimitedRecords, bddo:KeyValueHeader, bddo:DelimitedTable, bddo:TreeDocument))
        }""" ] .

# An ASCII-coded numeric field must have a width, a terminator or a separator, or it cannot be
# read. bddo:separatedBy is an extent mechanism in its own right -- the element is bounded by the
# runs of separator bytes either side of it -- and it is the ONLY one such a field may combine
# with none of the others: see bddo:FieldShape, which forbids pairing it with any size or with a
# terminator, because two answers to "where does this value end" is one too many.
# Exempt: a field of a delimited-record container gets its extent from the container's
# recordDelimiter/fieldDelimiter, not from its own size, terminator or separator.
bddo:AsciiNumericWidthShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:asciiInteger / bddo:asciiDecimal field must declare a width (size / sizeFromField / sizeFromExpression), a terminator, or bddo:separatedBy." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:dataType ?dt .
            FILTER(?dt IN (bddo:asciiInteger, bddo:asciiDecimal))
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this bddo:size ?s }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this bddo:sizeFromField ?f }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this bddo:sizeFromExpression ?e }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this bddo:terminator ?t }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this bddo:separatedBy ?sep }
            FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                ?p bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this .
                ?p a ?c .
                # Same exemption as bddo:VariableLengthFieldShape, and it must be kept in step
                # with it: both ask "does the container decide this field's extent?", and a new
                # container form has to be listed in both or a description conforms to one shape
                # and not the other.
                FILTER(?c IN (bddo:DelimitedRecords, bddo:KeyValueHeader, bddo:DelimitedTable,
                              bddo:TreeDocument))
            }
        }""" ] .

# numericBase is meaningful only for :asciiInteger.
bddo:NumericBaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:numericBase ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:numericBase ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:in ( 8 10 16 ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:numericBase must be 8, 10 or 16." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:dataType ; sh:hasValue bddo:asciiInteger ;
        sh:message "bddo:numericBase applies only to a field whose bddo:dataType is bddo:asciiInteger." ] .

bddo:EndiannessRuleShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:EndiannessRule ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:condition ; sh:or ( [ sh:datatype xsd:string ] [ sh:datatype bddo:HelExpression ] ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:message "bddo:condition takes exactly one value, a HEL expression (an xsd:string or bddo:HelExpression literal)." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:ruleEndianness ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:BigEndian bddo:LittleEndian ) ;
        sh:message "bddo:ruleEndianness must be bddo:BigEndian or bddo:LittleEndian." ] .

bddo:ConditionalEndiannessShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:hasConditionalEndianness ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasConditionalEndianness ; sh:maxCount 1 ; sh:nodeKind sh:BlankNodeOrIRI ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasConditionalEndianness must point to a single rdf:List." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:endianness ; sh:maxCount 0 ;
        sh:message "bddo:endianness and bddo:hasConditionalEndianness are mutually exclusive." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasConditionalEndianness [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:class bddo:EndiannessRule ;
        sh:message "Every member of a hasConditionalEndianness list must be a bddo:EndiannessRule." ] .

bddo:DelimitedRecordsShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DelimitedRecords ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:recordDelimiter ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:recordDelimiter takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:fieldDelimiter ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:fieldDelimiter takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:whitespaceSeparated ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:whitespaceSeparated takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] ;
    # A run of whitespace and a fixed delimiter are two different separation rules.
    sh:not [ sh:and ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:fieldDelimiter ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
                      [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:whitespaceSeparated ; sh:hasValue true ] ] ) ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyValueSeparator ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:keyValueSeparator takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:quoteChar ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:quoteChar takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:escapeChar ; sh:datatype xsd:hexBinary ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:escapeChar takes at most one value; each is an xsd:hexBinary literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:commentPrefix ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:commentPrefix takes at most one value; each is an xsd:string literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:skipRecords ; sh:minInclusive 0 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:skipRecords takes at most one value; each is a number at least 0." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:trimWhitespace ; sh:datatype xsd:boolean ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:trimWhitespace takes at most one value; each is an xsd:boolean literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasField ; sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:DelimitedRecords must declare at least one field." ] .

# A KeyValueHeader needs a separator, and each of its fields is located at most one way. A field
# with neither locator is located by its simple key (Processing Model, key/value headers), which
# is what an HDL compiler emits for an unquoted header field name.
bddo:KeyValueHeaderShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:KeyValueHeader ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyValueSeparator ; sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:KeyValueHeader must declare bddo:keyValueSeparator." ] ;
    # At most one locator per field: bddo:key in a flat header, bddo:keyPath in a nested one, or
    # neither, when the field is located by its simple key.
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasField [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:node [ sh:xone (
            [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:key ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
              sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyPath ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ]
            [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyPath ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
              sh:property [ sh:path bddo:key ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ]
            [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:key ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ;
              sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyPath ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ] ) ;
          sh:message "Every field of a bddo:KeyValueHeader declares at most one bddo:key (flat) or one bddo:keyPath (nested), never both; a field with neither is located by its simple key." ] ;
        sh:message "Every field of a bddo:KeyValueHeader declares at most one bddo:key (flat) or one bddo:keyPath (nested), never both; a field with neither is located by its simple key." ] .

# ---------- Tree documents ----------
bddo:TreeDocumentShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:TreeDocument ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:treeSyntax ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:xml bddo:json ) ;
        sh:message "A bddo:TreeDocument must declare exactly one bddo:treeSyntax: bddo:xml or bddo:json." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasNamespaceBinding ; sh:class bddo:NamespaceBinding ;
        sh:message "Each value of bddo:hasNamespaceBinding is an instance of bddo:NamespaceBinding." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasField ; sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A bddo:TreeDocument must declare at least one field." ] ;
    # Every field is located by a path, and by nothing else. A byte offset or a record key in a
    # tree document is a leftover from another container form and would address nothing.
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasField [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [ sh:path bddo:nodePath ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
            sh:property [ sh:path bddo:key ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ;
            sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyPath ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ;
            sh:property [ sh:path bddo:atOffset ; sh:maxCount 0 ] ;
            sh:message "Every field of a bddo:TreeDocument is located by exactly one bddo:nodePath, and not by a key, a key path or a byte offset." ] ;
        sh:message "Every field of a bddo:TreeDocument is located by exactly one bddo:nodePath, and not by a key, a key path or a byte offset." ] ;
    # Namespaces bind prefixes in XPath. JSON Pointer has no prefixes to bind, so a binding on a
    # JSON document is a misunderstanding worth catching rather than ignoring.
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "bddo:hasNamespaceBinding applies to an XML tree document; JSON Pointer has no namespace prefixes." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:treeSyntax bddo:json .
            $this bddo:hasNamespaceBinding ?b .
        }""" ] .

bddo:NamespaceBindingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:NamespaceBinding ;
    sh:targetObjectsOf bddo:hasNamespaceBinding ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:namespacePrefix , bddo:namespaceIRI ;
    # The prefix MAY be the empty string -- that is how a default namespace is bound -- so
    # minCount rather than a non-blank check.
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:namespacePrefix ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:namespacePrefix takes exactly one value, an xsd:string literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:namespaceIRI ; sh:datatype xsd:anyURI ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A namespace binding needs the IRI it binds to; a prefix alone names nothing." ] ;
    # XML Namespaces: a namespace name is an absolute IRI (a relative one is deprecated and
    # compares as a string, so two documents could disagree about it).
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:namespaceIRI ; sh:pattern "^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*:" ;
        sh:message "bddo:namespaceIRI must be an absolute IRI (it begins with a scheme)." ] .

# The prefixes a tree document's XML paths use: each bound once, and every one used is bound.
bddo:TreePrefixBindingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    rdfs:comment "An XML tree document binds each namespace prefix at most once, and every prefix its fields' bddo:nodePath values use is bound by one of its bddo:hasNamespaceBinding entries; only xml is predeclared."@en ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:TreeDocument ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:hasNamespaceBinding ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "Two bddo:hasNamespaceBinding entries of one tree document bind the prefix '{?px}'; a prefix is bound at most once." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?px WHERE {
            $this bddo:hasNamespaceBinding ?a , ?b .
            ?a bddo:namespacePrefix ?px . ?b bddo:namespacePrefix ?px .
            FILTER(!sameTerm(?a, ?b))
        }""" ] ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A bddo:nodePath of this XML tree document ({?p}) uses a prefix that no bddo:hasNamespaceBinding binds (only xml is predeclared)." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?p WHERE {
            { SELECT $this (GROUP_CONCAT(?px; separator="|") AS ?alts) WHERE {
                $this bddo:treeSyntax bddo:xml .
                OPTIONAL { $this bddo:hasNamespaceBinding/bddo:namespacePrefix ?px . FILTER(?px != "") }
            } GROUP BY $this }
            $this bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first ?f .
            ?f bddo:nodePath ?p .
            # Each bound prefix is matched literally: an NCName may hold '.' and '-', which a
            # regular expression reads as metacharacters, so each is put in a class of its own.
            BIND(REPLACE(?alts, "([.-])", "[$1]") AS ?quoted)
            BIND(REPLACE(?p, CONCAT("(^|[/@])(", IF(?quoted != "", CONCAT(?quoted, "|"), ""), "xml):"), "$1") AS ?rest)
            FILTER(REGEX(?rest, "(^|[/@])[^/@:]+:"))
        }""" ] .

# A path outside a tree document addresses nothing.
bddo:NodePathShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:targetSubjectsOf bddo:nodePath ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:nodePath ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:nodePath takes at most one value; each is an xsd:string literal." ] ;
    # Path syntax by the container's tree syntax: a JSON Pointer (RFC 6901), or the abbreviated
    # XPath of the Processing Model (child steps, 1-based positional predicates, one final
    # attribute step). Names are any run of characters that are not path punctuation, so every
    # XML name matches; what a name means is resolved at load time.
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "This bddo:nodePath ({?p}) is not a JSON Pointer (RFC 6901): it must be empty or a sequence of /-prefixed reference tokens, with ~ only in ~0 or ~1." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?p WHERE {
            $this bddo:nodePath ?p .
            ?container bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this ; bddo:treeSyntax bddo:json .
            FILTER(!REGEX(?p, "^(/([^~/]|~[01])*)*$"))
        }""" ] ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "This bddo:nodePath ({?p}) is not an abbreviated XPath of child steps, optional [n] positions (n >= 1) and at most one final @attribute step." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?p WHERE {
            $this bddo:nodePath ?p .
            ?container bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this ; bddo:treeSyntax bddo:xml .
            FILTER(!REGEX(?p, "^(/?([^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+:)?[^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+(\\\\[[1-9][0-9]*\\\\])?(/([^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+:)?[^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+(\\\\[[1-9][0-9]*\\\\])?)*(/@([^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+:)?[^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+)?|@([^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+:)?[^/@\\\\[\\\\]:\\\\s]+)$"))
        }""" ] ;
    # Every container, not just one: a field shared by a tree document and a binary struct would
    # otherwise pass, and the struct would read a field it cannot locate.
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "bddo:nodePath locates a node in a parsed tree, so every container of the field must be a bddo:TreeDocument ({?container} is not, or there is none)." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this ?container WHERE {
            $this bddo:nodePath ?p .
            {
                FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?any bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this }
            } UNION {
                ?container bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?container a bddo:TreeDocument }
            }
        }""" ] .

# ---------- Nested records ----------
bddo:RecordGroupingShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:RecordGrouping ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:groupingStyle ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( bddo:keyedGroup bddo:valuedGroup bddo:sectionHeaded ) ;
        sh:message "A bddo:RecordGrouping must name exactly one style: keyedGroup, valuedGroup or sectionHeaded." ] ;
    # Both tokens, always. A grouping that says where a group starts and not where it ends
    # cannot be read: every record after the first group would be inside it.
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:groupOpenToken ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:groupOpenToken takes exactly one value, an xsd:string literal." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:groupCloseToken ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:groupCloseToken takes exactly one value, an xsd:string literal." ] .

bddo:GroupedRecordsShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DelimitedRecords ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:hasGrouping ; sh:class bddo:RecordGrouping ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:hasGrouping takes at most one value; each is an instance of bddo:RecordGrouping." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:pathSeparator ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:pathSeparator takes at most one value; each is an xsd:string literal." ] .

# A path has nothing to traverse unless its container nests.
bddo:KeyPathShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyPath ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "bddo:keyPath takes at most one value; each is an xsd:string literal." ] ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "bddo:keyPath locates a field inside nested groups, so its container must declare bddo:hasGrouping; without one the container is flat and the path addresses nothing." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:keyPath ?path .
            FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                ?container bddo:hasField/rdf:rest*/rdf:first $this .
                ?container bddo:hasGrouping ?g .
            }
        }""" ] .

# bddo:key is meaningful only inside a KeyValueHeader; a table's columns are positional.
bddo:DelimitedTableShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:DelimitedTable ;
    sh:or ( [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:fieldDelimiter ; sh:minCount 1 ] ]
            [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:whitespaceSeparated ; sh:hasValue true ] ] ) ;
    sh:property [ sh:path bddo:keyValueSeparator ; sh:maxCount 0 ;
        sh:message "bddo:keyValueSeparator applies to a KeyValueHeader, not a DelimitedTable." ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path ( bddo:hasField [ sh:zeroOrMorePath rdf:rest ] rdf:first ) ;
        sh:node [ sh:property [ sh:path bddo:key ; sh:maxCount 0 ;
            sh:message "bddo:key applies to a field of a KeyValueHeader; a DelimitedTable's columns are located by position." ] ] ;
        sh:message "No field of a bddo:DelimitedTable states bddo:key: a table's columns are located by position, and bddo:key applies to a field of a KeyValueHeader." ] .

# A varint's length comes from the data, so declaring a width contradicts it.
bddo:VarintWidthShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass bddo:Field ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:message "A variable-length integer field (bddo:varuint, bddo:varint, bddo:sqliteVarint) must not declare a width or terminator: its length is discovered from the bytes." ;
        sh:prefixes bddo:_prefixes ;
        sh:select """SELECT $this WHERE {
            $this bddo:dataType ?dt .
            FILTER(?dt IN (bddo:varuint, bddo:varint, bddo:sqliteVarint))
            $this ?p ?v .
            FILTER(?p IN (bddo:size, bddo:sizeFromField, bddo:sizeFromExpression,
                          bddo:sizeToEndOfStream, bddo:terminator, bddo:bitLength))
        }""" ] .

# ---------- Range datatype shapes ----------
# Each XSD rdfs:range above, enforced wherever the property is used: activation is by
# subjects-of, so an untyped subject is checked too (tools/test_range_datatypes.py).
# Integer ranges accept any XSD integer-family datatype within the range's bounds.

Examples

Conditional Data Type Dispatch (PNG chunk)

A generic chunk is parsed first; the ChunkData field is then re-interpreted using a more specific structure based on ChunkType. This is the type-length-value (TLV) pattern.

@prefix bddo: <https://hexplain.io/ns/bddo#> .
@prefix png:  <https://hexplain.io/formats/png#> .

png:Chunk a bddo:Struct ;
    bddo:endianness bddo:BigEndian ;
    bddo:hasField ( png:ChunkLength png:ChunkType png:ChunkData png:ChunkCRC ) .

png:ChunkLength a bddo:Field ; bddo:dataType bddo:uint32 .
png:ChunkType   a bddo:Field ; bddo:dataType bddo:string ; bddo:size 4 ; bddo:encoding bddo:ascii .

png:ChunkData a bddo:Field ;
    bddo:dataType bddo:bytes ;
    bddo:sizeFromField png:ChunkLength ;
    bddo:hasConditionalDataType (
        [ a bddo:DataTypeRule ; bddo:condition "instance.parent.ChunkType == 'IHDR'" ; bddo:ruleDataType png:IHDR_ChunkData ]
        [ a bddo:DataTypeRule ; bddo:condition "instance.parent.ChunkType == 'PLTE'" ; bddo:ruleDataType png:PLTE_ChunkData ]
    ) .

# CRC-32 over the ChunkType and ChunkData bytes.
png:ChunkCRC a bddo:Field ;
    bddo:dataType bddo:uint32 ;
    bddo:checksum [ a bddo:Checksum ;
        bddo:checksumAlgorithm bddo:crc32 ;
        bddo:coversFromField png:ChunkType ;
        bddo:coversToField   png:ChunkData ] .

Count-based array, enumeration, and offset (sketch)

@prefix bddo: <https://hexplain.io/ns/bddo#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix tiff: <https://hexplain.io/formats/tiff#> .

# An IFD entry count followed by that many entries.
tiff:IFD a bddo:Struct ;
    bddo:hasField ( tiff:NumEntries tiff:Entries tiff:NextIFDOffset ) .
tiff:NumEntries a bddo:Field ; bddo:dataType bddo:uint16 .
tiff:Entries a bddo:Field ;
    bddo:dataType tiff:IFDEntry ;
    bddo:repeatCountFromField tiff:NumEntries .

# A colour-type byte surfaced as a symbol.
tiff:ColorType a bddo:Field ;
    bddo:dataType bddo:uint8 ;
    bddo:enumeration [ a bddo:Enumeration ; bddo:hasEnumValue
        [ a bddo:EnumValue ; bddo:enumRawValue 0 ; rdfs:label "Grayscale" ] ,
        [ a bddo:EnumValue ; bddo:enumRawValue 2 ; rdfs:label "RGB" ] ,
        [ a bddo:EnumValue ; bddo:enumRawValue 3 ; rdfs:label "Indexed" ] ] .

# A structure located by an absolute offset read from a header field.
tiff:FirstIFD a bddo:Field ;
    bddo:dataType tiff:IFD ;
    bddo:atOffsetFromField tiff:IFDOffset ;
    bddo:offsetBase bddo:streamStart .
